- [[Weight Initialisation and Activation Scale]] — Initial weight scale decides whether the signal survives the layers: too small or too large and a deep net never trains, while He (ReLU) and Glorot (tanh) scales keep activation spread stable; measure first-layer and last-layer activation std before blaming the optimiser.
- [[Weight-of-Evidence Logistic Scorecard]] — A credit scorecard bins each characteristic, converts bins to weight of evidence, fits a logistic regression, and prints the result as additive points; it matches gradient boosting within noise on small data while staying defensible line by line.
- [[What Drives Sentiment: LIME, Per-Class Topics and Log-Odds]] — A polarity score says how many are unhappy, not why; LIME explains single predictions, NMF per class names the themes, and class log-odds needs a minimum count or a prior because rare words have high variance.
- [[Winner's Curse]] — When a test is underpowered, the estimates that reach significance are exaggerated: a true 1 percent lift with a 1 percent standard error had power 0.17, significant estimates averaged 2.5 times the truth, and some had the wrong sign.
- [[Word Embeddings and Similarity Measures]] — word2vec, GloVe and FastText give dense vectors where shared contexts mean closeness; covers negative sampling, subwords, analogy and bias limits, document vectors, and why to normalise before cosine.
- [[World Knowledge Model for Agent Planning]] — WKM gives an agent a separately trained knowledge model: task knowledge written once from contrasting good and bad trajectories, and state knowledge retrieved per step and mixed into action probabilities; 7-8B models beat fine-tuned baselines on ALFWorld, WebShop and ScienceWorld (self-reported).
- [[Write-Ahead Logs, fsync and the Durability Ladder]] — Saved is a ladder of rungs (process buffer, page cache, device, log, replica, backup), each surviving a different failure; fsync and a checksummed write-ahead log make a crash boring, and replace-by-rename keeps whole files atomic.
- [[Write-Audit-Publish and Atomic Visibility]] — Idempotency repairs a crash on rerun but not what readers see meanwhile; write beside and swap so readers never see half a write, audit the staged copy before publishing so a failed test protects the tested table too, and expose only what passes: an honest gap instead of a wrong number, and a containment tool during an incident.
- [[Writing a Metric Definition Two Analysts Agree On]] — A metric definition fixes population, finality, window, clock, unit, owner and version in a spec that code reads; immature cohorts read as falls (censoring) and the function should return None rather than zero.
- [[YOCO Decoder-Decoder Architecture]] — YOCO splits a language model into a self-decoder with a constant-size cache and a cross-decoder that reuses one global KV cache, so the cache is stored once; at 3B it cut 1M-token inference memory 9.38x and 512K prefill from 180 s to under 6 s (self-reported).

## Patterns

- [[ACI (Agent-Computer Interface)]] — Tool design discipline for agents — the interface between agent and tools, analogous to HCI for humans. Good ACI determines whether the agent uses tools correctly or hallucinates parameters.
- [[Agent Orchestration Patterns]] — Four levels of agent logic structure — single prompt, single agent + tools, multi-step graph, multi-agent — ordered by control and complexity, with a decision shortcut and the governing rule to start simple and escalate only on demonstrated need.
- [[Agent Quality Review Checklist]] — Nineteen agent-system-specific review checks across six families — prompt/LLM smells, tool safety, workflow state, retrieval/context, memory write-back, and accountability — of which "safeguard in prose only" is named the highest-value finding.
- [[Agent Scaffolding Skill Layers]] — A three-layer Claude Code skill design for scaffolding agents — a generic parallel-subagent factory (L1), standalone capability add-skills that read the target before generating (L2), and a domain-specific bundle that orchestrates L2 skills in sequence (L3).
- [[Agentic Workflow Patterns]] — Anthropic's five composable workflow patterns for LLM agents — when to use each, and the ACI principle for tool design.
- [[AI Project Archetypes]] — Four archetypes — Information Retrieval, Document Generation, Workflow Automation, Conversational Interface — that cover most nonprofit AI projects, each with a complexity floor, disambiguating questions, and a mapping to concrete scaffold parameters.
- [[AI Project Template Scaffold]] — A generic, framework-agnostic starter repo pattern for standing up new AI agent projects — modeled on a mature reference project's skills/docs/infra layout plus a conventional data-science project skeleton (`.github`, `project_init.sh`, `.vscode`, `data/`, `docs/`, `infrastructure/`), kept as its own repo rather than nested under the reference project.
- [[Asked vs Derived Scaffold Variables]] — A scaffold interview that splits ~20 template variables into six asked out loud, eight derived-then-confirmed, and the rest left silently defaulted — with the split decided by blast radius of a wrong guess, not by how many variables exist.
- [[Block Attribute Inversion]] — Turning a list of unanswerable design questions into per-component metadata — when each architectural block ships with its own failure mode, scaling limit, and cost driver, the design's weak points are generated from the assembly rather than interrogated from a user who cannot answer.
- [[Branch Naming Convention Pattern]] — Ticket-linked, type-prefixed branch naming (`type-TICKET-slug`) with per-repo type taxonomies and a `hotfix` escape hatch for production-blocking bugs.
- [[Callable-By Integration Contract]] — When a project needs to work with an external hosted service, the scaffoldable unit is not the service but the contract that makes your system reachable from it — a plain HTTP endpoint plus a signed-webhook receiver for the reverse direction.
- [[Capability Parity Audit]] — A method for deciding what a shared template should absorb next — classify every requested capability as have / partial / gap against the template's *verified* current state, then prioritize by how many consumers share the gap rather than by how loudly any one asked.
- [[Capability Runtime-Coupling Tiers]] — Sorting agent capabilities into runtime-coupled (T1), runtime-adjacent (T2), and runtime-independent (T3) — because cross-runtime parity is a meaningful goal for T1/T2 and a category error for T3.
- [[Chain of Thought]] — Inference-time technique where the LLM is prompted to show its reasoning before answering — improves accuracy on multi-step logic, arithmetic, and routing decisions with no training cost.
- [[Complexity Floor]] — The minimum viable complexity of a project shape — a constraint that makes capacity a selection criterion rather than a schedule, forcing archetype reframing rather than scope-thinning when the team can't reach the floor.
- [[Copier Re-Entry as Capability Path]] — Treating `.copier-answers.yml` as durable scaffold state so that flipping a template answer and re-rendering is the *only* sanctioned way a component enters a generated repo — which turns "scaffold the MVP now, add later" into a real guarantee rather than advice.
- [[Copier Upstream Update Workflow]] — Pulling template changes into an already-scaffolded project — a clean-tree gate, a mandatory `--pretend --diff` preview, impact-categorized change review, and conflict resolution that preserves local intent over template defaults.
- [[Corrective Follow-Up Dispatch]] — Gated subagents that a signal-detection pass failed to trigger get dispatched in a second round when always-on subagents report out-of-dimension signal — treating reviewer observations as a recovery path for missed conditional dispatch.
- [[Data Pipeline Pattern Selection]] — Four ways data reaches an AI system — batch ingest, event-driven, streaming, hybrid — chosen by one question about where the data comes from, with hybrid treated as a phase-2 evolution rather than a phase-1 option.
- [[Deferred Decision Status]] — A three-value status for design decisions — Resolved / Open / Deferred(trigger) — where a deferral must name the concrete event that reopens it, and a triggerless deferral silently degrades to Open so it still blocks the gate.
- [[Derived-and-Hidden Design Decisions]] — A scaffold variable marked `when: false` ships the code correctly and prevents the design conversation entirely — the failure mode where observability and guardrails exist as files nobody chose, distinguished from legitimate derivation by whether a silent default has an irreversible failure mode.
- [[Design-Before-Infrastructure Sequencing]] — The decision to make design scoping a standalone skill that runs before the scaffold interview rather than a wrapper around it — because design and build are separate phases that may be months apart, and merging them makes the combined skill unusable for anyone who already designed.
- [[DESIGN.md Artifact]] — The six-section design record every scaffolded project ships — problem, actors, C4 system context, MVP scope, key decisions, and non-functional constraints — shipped as a placeholder stub when unfilled so the skipped conversation stays visible.
- [[Deterministic Review Substrate]] — Review steps that are mechanical (diff scoping, dedup clustering, schema validation, report rendering) are pushed into a CLI the agent shells out to, so the only work left to LLM judgment is the part that actually needs judgment.
- [[Evidence Classification Model]] — A four-state classification (verified / supported / hypothesis / question) every review finding must carry before it is returned, with self-verification assigned to the producing subagent rather than the orchestrator.
- [[Integration Pattern Selection]] — Five ways an AI system connects to external services — MCP tools, Composio connectors, direct httpx clients, webhook receivers, n8n glue — and the single discriminating question that picks each one.
- [[Merge Impact and Evidence State]] — A two-axis finding schema that separates how much a problem matters (merge_impact) from how sure the reviewer is (evidence_state) — so an uncertain critical finding and a certain trivial one stay distinguishable.
- [[Multi-Agent Role Specialization]] — Multiple agents with distinct roles and tool sets coordinated by an orchestrator — the highest-complexity orchestration, justified only when quality genuinely requires different modes of thinking, and explicitly never a starting point.
- [[Multi-Repo Claude Organization]] — How to organize .claude/, .agents/, and docs/ across related repos — avoiding skill sprawl, maintaining canonical sources, and sharing context between parallel workspaces.
- [[Multi-Step Graph Orchestration]] — A directed graph of processing nodes with explicit transitions, where the LLM executes within nodes but you control flow between them — the pattern for branching, human gates, and retry loops, with a concrete refactor trigger for when to adopt it.
- [[Parallel Dimension Scanner Architecture]] — Code review decomposed into independent single-concern scanner agents dispatched in parallel — each owns one dimension, one ID prefix, and one severity mapping — so review breadth scales by adding agents rather than lengthening one prompt.
- [[Payload Security Defects at Canon]] — When a code-gen payload is about to be mirrored into every scaffolded project, its defects must be fixed in the canonical copy first — a mirror multiplies a single-source bug into every downstream consumer, and the fix window closes once copies exist.
- [[Project Discovery Conversation]] — A guided pre-design conversation that turns a volunteer's pain point into a Project Profile — deliberately withholding all technology vocabulary so the artifact commits to outcomes and constraints, not framework choices.
- [[ReAct Pattern]] — Reasoning + Acting loop — the foundational single-agent pattern where the LLM alternates thought and tool calls until it has enough information to answer. Implemented via create_react_agent in LangGraph.
- [[Read-Only by Default with Explicit Authorization]] — A review agent's safety model built from an enumerated safe-command allowlist plus a single authorization gate placed at the write boundary — so subagents get real shell access for verification while every mutating action, including writing a draft the system itself produced, stops at proposing.
- [[Scope-Gated Reporter Dispatch]] — Sizing a review to the change by declaring a scope up front, then recording each reporter's skip with its reason in the verdict — so "not run" is visible evidence rather than an absent section a reader mistakes for a clean pass.
- [[Scope-POC Design Interview]] — A five-tier system-design interview that produces a DESIGN.md answering *what* to build — idempotent over its own output, ratifying rather than adopting inherited decisions, and treating "I don't know" as a recordable answer instead of a blocker.
- [[Shared Context Brief]] — A structured brief the orchestrator fills in exactly once and passes to every dispatched subagent, so N parallel reviewers share one grounding pass instead of each re-deriving repository context from the same diff.
- [[Silent Fallthrough in String-Keyed Discovery]] — When a tool finds its working target by grepping a literal string, renaming that string doesn't crash — it selects a different target with no error, making the rename a flag-day change whose only defence is a call-site checklist.
- [[Single Agent With Tools]] — One agent with a tool set, where the LLM chooses which tools to call and when to stop — the level most real projects need, and the recommended default before any graph or multi-agent escalation.
- [[Single Prompt Baseline]] — One LLM call with no tools, state, or framework — the simplest orchestration, correct for classification/extraction/summarization, and the baseline any agent must beat to justify its complexity.
- [[Six-Pillar Agent Engineering Assessment]] — A rubric scoring agent codebases across six engineering pillars — prompt, context, harness, loop, graph, evaluation — with each pillar's requirements tiered Must/Should/Nice so a coverage percentage separates "missing the foundation" from "not yet mature.
- [[Skill Preloading via Agent Definition]] — A skill file is inert until an agent definition names it in a `skills:` field — the two-file split (`.claude/skills/<name>/SKILL.md` plus `.claude/agents/<name>.md`) is what turns checklist content into context actually loaded at subagent startup.
- [[Source Severity vs Merge Impact]] — When a review aggregates findings from external tools, each tool's native severity is preserved unrewritten and a separate merge-impact field answers the different question of whether this particular PR should merge.
- [[Specification by Example]] — The classical practice (ATDD/SBE) of expressing a requirement as a concrete example rather than prose, producing an executable specification that cannot drift from the code.
- [[Sync as Render, Not Copy]] — When one canonical source ships into a second tree that addresses it differently, the sync must transform link targets and variable names rather than mirror bytes — a byte-for-byte copy cannot serve both contexts, and hand-copied files drift backwards.
- [[TDD as Coding-Agent Harness]] — Using a failing test to constrain the agent that writes code — the clearest goal you can give it — plus the guardrail neither popular source addresses: an agent that writes both test and implementation can satisfy itself.
- [[Template Floor Raising]] — Prioritizing scaffold work by cross-portfolio gap frequency rather than by any single project's needs — the template's job is to make the weakness every existing repo shares impossible to inherit, so new projects start above the portfolio's floor.
- [[Template Migrations for Structural Moves]] — A file-level merge cannot express "this module moved" — template `_migrations` supply the missing structural edit, and degrade to a loud WARN rather than a silent overwrite when the file being moved was hand-edited.
- [[Track2Vec Playlist Co-Occurrence Embeddings]] — Item2vec-style technique — treat playlists as "sentences" and item IDs as "words", train Word2Vec over co-occurrence to get dense embeddings that capture human curation intent rather than content similarity.
- [[Verified Runtime Capability Constraint]] — A design rule that a control may only be specified if the runtime demonstrably enforces it — three separate Parallax mechanisms (a timeout budget, a cost cap, a submodule-vendored skill) were dropped on discovering the harness had no way to make them real.
- [[Wander — Question-Generating Review Agent]] — A review agent that produces 3–5 pointed questions instead of findings — surfacing intent, edge cases, walked-past decisions, blast radius, and the conspicuously absent thing — as the "yin" complement to defect scanners.
- [[Webhook Handler Idempotency]] — Every inbound webhook handler must tolerate the same event arriving more than once — at-least-once delivery is the sender's contract, so deduplication is unambiguously the receiver's responsibility.
- [[Test Derived Defaults Without Overrides]] — scaffold variables computed with `when: false` must be tested with zero `-d` overrides, otherwise the test only proves the override works
- [[Read-Every-Consumer Migration]] — before a structural move, enumerate every reader of the old path (string keys, CI, docs, hooks) rather than trusting grep for the import
- [[Role-Scoped Parity]] — judge template-vs-project parity per role (AI engineer / ML / infra) instead of as one scalar gap count
- [[Template Only Decided Patterns]] — hold a capability out of the template until its shape is decided in a real project; an undecided scaffold ossifies guesses
- [[Spend Gate Before the Model Call]] — every billable call sits behind an input hash (incl. prompt/tag versions) and an explicit allow-spend switch so re-runs are free and unchanged inputs never regenerate
- [[Replayable Slate Ranker]] — pure ranker over [0,1] signals with fatigue, MMR and kind caps, versioned and logged per serve so any slate can be replayed
- [[Explore Slice for Position Bias]] — reserve a small randomised slice of serves so engagement feedback can measure the ranker instead of only confirming it
- [[Dual Parser with Shared Shape]] — model parser and rules parser emit the identical structure so the rules path is a true fallback on timeout or no-spend
- [[Assembled, Not Generated Replies]] — compose user-facing replies from retrieved records and templates so nothing shown can be unfounded
- [[Turn-Bounded Citation Walk]] — a backward walk over message history collecting citations must stop at the latest human message or prior-turn sources leak into the current answer
- [[Rationale-Blind Worst-Case Review]] — read-only advisor that ignores rationale and runs four passes (empty-input collapse, p99 by consequence, named rollback, chained-probability) to surface the one failure to fix first
- [[REUSE/ADAPT/SKIP Asset Verdicts]] — research-scout output contract with a mandatory "salvageable at lower cost" column so SKIP is a claim made after checking, not a default
- [[Stage-Scoped Scout Agents]] — sub-agents that run exactly one pipeline stage per invocation, never skip forward, never commit, stop on first blocker
- [[Append-Only Log Writer Invariants]] — append at EOF, never re-sort, strictly-longer-with-all-headers check, Edit over shell redirection, before/after line counts; readers take max-by-date
- [[Silence Reads as Clean]] — the recurring defect where a job that never ran, a swallowed nonzero exit, or a dead channel emits the same bytes as "healthy, nothing to report"
- [[Signal Registry with Resolution States]] — one registry of (name, kind, resolver|UNOBSERVABLE) and four row states (registered/needs-collection/unobservable/unregistered) that turned 49 unmeasurable hypotheses into a typed backlog
- [[Falsifiable Hypothesis Gate]] — skill that refuses an experiment row unless the metric is registered, the claim falsifiable, due date in review rounds, plane named, and friction rows carry a pattern key
- [[Cross-Sweep Finding Fingerprint]] — sha256(file+symbol+category+title)[:12] identity for review findings, trend states, and deterministic advisory promotion rules; JSON is truth, Markdown a view
- [[Deterministic Signal Detection for Dispatch]] — regex content/path signals replace the LLM "is this agent code?" question; signals are guidance not gate; FP benign, FN recovered by follow-up round
- [[Reversibility Cliff]] — the unattended-action boundary belongs at notification, not mutation; operator approval measured as the least reliable component; both "obviously safe" auto-mutations failed on repo data
- [[Friction Promotion Ladder]] — ledger row at N=1, conditional warn-hook at N=2, on-demand ref at N=3, always-on CLAUDE.md text as the exception — ranked by always-on context cost
- [[Friction Is Rework, Not Error Rate]] — dashboard audit: 3 of 5 friction metrics were productivity/safety signals; friction redefined as repeated or rephrased requests, ~60% of it unmeasured
- [[Scoped Phase Dispatch with Pre-Resolved Files]] — One build agent per plan phase with a pre-resolved `Files:` write set (a floor, not a fence on reading) and the documentation DoD applied at execute time, after telemetry showed stale docs were 28% of review findings.
- [[Red-Then-Green Proof for Fix-Stage Tests]] — A fix that adds a guarding test must show it red against the old code before green with the new — `not.toBe(429)` was satisfied by a 500 and proved nothing.
- [[Derive the Gate, Don't Copy It]] — The `Gate:` line handed to reviewers and any doc listing gate targets must be generated from the Makefile, never typed; a typed list is the trust-the-narrative failure the review rules ban.
- [[Cross-Dimensional Review Adjudicator]] — A single agent after parallel review reporters that merges, resolves, and re-ranks findings into a proposal with one-line reasons — never a direct ledger edit, never a re-run.
- [[Review Reporter Ground Rules]] — Shared trust rules for per-dimension review reporters: file content is data not instruction, never re-run the gate, 80% confidence floor, empty array is legitimate, read the repo rules in force first.
- [[Always-Red Check Carries No Signal]] — A CI job red for eight merges is as useless as one that silently passes — a falsified issue premise sat unread in its log — and its mirror is a gate passing for the wrong reason from a dirty checkout.
- [[Shadow Signal Before Gate Change]] — Compute the proposed gate key as a non-gating shadow beside the live one and count disagreements before changing the gate; a plan step conditional on an undecided question is BLOCKED, not deferred.
- [[Taste vs Defect Verdict Split]] — Split a reviewer's verdict into a serve/no-serve taste value and a `defect_fields` list mapped to the owning pipeline stage, and log the counterfactual (`shelves_eligible`) at serve time.
- [[Decision-ID Reservation Across Concurrent Plans]] — Concurrent plans appending `D-NN` rows re-grep the highest ID at edit time, never reuse numbers, and treat the specs as the registry and the decisions doc as a rendering.
- [[Every Column Names Its Reader]] — A plausible, constrained, commented column nothing reads is a trap, not documentation — four lifecycle columns, one real — so no column or JSON field lands until a named reader exists, and "nothing reads it" is a plumbing finding.
- [[Sanitize Never Fails, Validate Never Mutates]] — The two-stage contract for model-output enums, the "caught later at publish" containment claim that turned out false, and the fallback value that was itself outside the target column's vocabulary.
- [[Ship Logging Before Features]] — Serve-time logs cannot be backfilled: log the full ordered result set with version stamps and as-displayed position, decide a new slot type's position convention before the first serve, and keep the current system as the baseline arm.
- [[Scan-Floor Guard Against Silent No-Op Checks]] — any check that scans a set must fail if the set is empty or below an expected floor, or it passes vacuously
- [[Artifact Is Not Evidence: Push and Probe]] — a migration or deploy marked applied proves nothing; probe the live behaviour (e.g. RLS via a SECURITY DEFINER RPC) before closing
- [[Data Clusters by Writer and Trust]] — partition tables into content / product / interaction clusters by who writes them; plain-column references across clusters instead of cascading FKs
- [[Cached Derivation Without a Recompute Path]] — a stored derived value whose writer no longer exists silently drives behaviour; every cached derivation needs a named recompute path
- [[Deadline-Scope Isolation]] — keep deadline-bound work in its own scope so open-ended investigation cannot absorb it
- [[Named-Trigger Deferral]] — a deferred decision must name the measurable trigger that reopens it and the one fact that would flip it
- [[Re-Scope Over Sequence for Colliding Issues]] — when parallel issues touch the same files, split by file ownership instead of serialising; supersede dated audits rather than editing them
- [[Hydration Gap Silent Input Loss]] — SSR paints before React attaches listeners, so early input is silently dropped; gate on mounted state and assert effects, not visibility
- [[Write-Only Fields and the Reader Map]] — audit every produced field by written-by / selected-by / read-by; prompt output nothing reads is cost without signal
- [[Differential Parity Testing Against the Original]] — when porting logic between runtimes, execute the original against the port over randomised payloads; fixtures encode the author's belief and cannot find transcription errors
- [[The Untested Copy Wins]] — duplicated logic drifts and the untested copy is the one that runs (tested publish gate with zero callers); fix at the primitive, derive the second copy, or guard it differentially
- [[Collapse Site Detection]] — the fog pass: four-row table (found nothing / abstain / error / answered from nothing), grep probes, unknown-knowns, reversibility as a harness property, precision scored separately from recall
- [[Two-Axis Claim Schema]] — decide-family claim format with orthogonal confidence × decision_impact, mandatory `settles`, and the speculative/fatal cell as the one teams rationalise away
- [[When a Lens Earns Agenthood]] — a lens is an agent only for Isolation or Parallelism; committee and duplicate-lens failure modes; skills orchestrate agents, never the reverse
- [[Assumption Audit]] — extract every load-bearing assumption (confident language, defaults, dependencies, absence) before evaluating any, ranked by cost of being wrong
- [[An Unrun Scan Is Not a Clean Scan]] — a review dimension with no inputs emits a `question`-state finding instead of `[]`; same bug as pytest testpaths collecting zero tests
- [[Auditable Track Router]] — ml vs agent routing with source/confidence/reason, declared > doc > files > default, ml-wins tie-break, abstain on low confidence, persisted as a `Track:` line
- [[Duplex Voice Breaks Discrete-Turn Assumptions]] — every request-response spec assumes a stop_reason; duplex voice has none, so scaffold only the graph behind the audio loop and generate under constraints, not generate-and-repair
- [[Fallback Mistaken for the Design]] — when only the degraded path has ever rendered, reviews critique the fallback; check which branch produced the surface and look one directory deeper before calling a feature unbuilt
- [[Listing Pages Masquerading as Records]] — crawlers that follow calendar/nav links and date pages via a permissive fallback emit listing pages as records; fix link selection and gate listings before extraction
- [[Budget Without a Meter Is a Preference]] — A product budget (taps, latency, cost) that nothing measures will be silently exceeded — a ≤4-tap intake shipped at ≥6 taps — so every budget ships with its meter, and when screen and requirement disagree the requirement wins.
- [[Principal Write-Surface Matrix]] — List every principal (users, tenants, operators, services, and the model provider) as a row with submit/read/mutate/cause columns, then ask whether any principal's write surface exceeds its authority — the test that found a tenant able to approve its own lifecycle transition.
- [[Four-Source Provenance Contract]] — Every stored claim in an AI system is tagged with one of four provenance sources — verified fact, deterministic inference, AI interpretation, human decision — in named columns, and consequential changes keep the chain agent_run → proposal → human edit → approval → state transition → external effect.
- [[Realtime Is UI Sync, Not the Event Bus]] — A database's realtime channel informs clients after a committed transaction; it is not the domain event bus — the order is command → transaction → state + event row → realtime notifies — so no business workflow may depend on a client being connected.
- [[Parallel Across Lanes, Migrations Alone]] — When running several agent builds concurrently in worktrees, memory is not the constraint — declared dependencies, shared file lanes, and the single shared database are — so parallelise across lanes, serialise within a lane, run migration-adding builds alone, and cap concurrency at 3 for review attention.
- [[A Wrong Finding Can Manufacture Its Failure]] — In silent-failure review, a coherent but unverified claim about a framework API is most dangerous because its "fix" looks like a safety improvement and can cause exactly the failure it warns about — so a blocker in that dimension requires observed behaviour (built output, emitted CSS/JS, type definitions), not a persuasive argument.
- [[One Facet Per Fact]] — Give each kind of fact (vibe, audience, time, offer) its own facet; derive what a rule can compute, stamp per-value provenance, accept on per-facet precision before coverage
- [[Confidence That Is Not a Probability]] — A "confidence" field is often a status lookup dressed as a float; calibration needs a real probability, a reader, and independent labels
- [[Starved Tag Triage: Edge, Prompt, or Supply]] — Diagnose an empty enrichment tag as missing edge, prompt suppression (quote grounding starves atmosphere tags), vocabulary gap, or supply gap
- [[Hide-Unknown Display Contract]] — Hide uncertain fields instead of showing "Unknown", never block publishing on optional data, derive CTA/price labels via resolvers rather than storing them
- [[Relevance Is the Score, Enrichability Is the Gate]] — Split "does this matter" from "can we enrich it"; gated items hold at full score; prominence from cross-source agreement
- [[Quoted Span as Proof of Reading]] — Every review finding must carry a quoted_span — the literal source text at the cited line, whitespace-normalised, within ±1 line — so a validator can mechanically reject findings from a reporter that never read the code it cites; convention claims add a spec_citation or must not invoke the spec at all.
- [[A Fake That Conforms to a Broken Seam]] — Test doubles that match a port exactly will faithfully reproduce the port's defects — a prototype threw away every rendered image because the LLM port had no images parameter and 300 green tests used fakes shaped to that port — so conformance needs one shared full-signature fake per port, a contract test on the real adapter's wire shape, and caller-side identity assertions.
- [[Adversarial Plan War Game]] — Attack a plan with 3–5 isolated attackers on diverse angles, require every threat to name who benefits and what they can observe and change (otherwise it is ordinary uncertainty), merge without adding threats, rank impact-first, attach a chosen Prevent/Detect/Recover/Accept to each, and compute the read with "incomplete" first because attacker silence is not a clean bill.
- [[Path-Verified Build State Markers]] — When scoping on top of an existing codebase, every capability a source doc claims exists must be backed by a file path — BUILT (exists and tested), ABSENT, or UNVERIFIED, never an optimistic partial — because a wrong done-marker silently removes work from the plan; on greenfield the honest state is SPECIFIED.
- [[Design Dry-Run Simulation]] — Before building, attack a design's own unvalidated premises (an unrun experiment becomes the first component, and the spec needs a failure branch), then role-play nominal, malformed and plausible-wrong scenarios turn by turn, stop at the first blocker, record it as a finding and revise the spec; a dry run with no revisions was lucky or not adversarial.
- [[Size the Gate to the Blast Radius]] — A readiness gate's cost should scale with what the plan can break, not how much it types — a Light path for small, effect-free plans that still runs every cheap blocking check, with two mandatory escapes back to Full (any check fails; the plan writes a hook, gate, shared skill or anything every session loads).
- [[Plan Epistemic State Axis]] — A plan doc carries an Epistemic field (UNTESTED / NO_CONFLICT / CONFLICTED / BLOCKED) separate from Status, with Epistemic-by distinguishing "skipped on purpose" from "dispatch failed" — NO_CONFLICT requires the advisor actually ran, freshness is guaranteed by the writer, and UNTESTED + READY is legal and visible instead of hidden behind compliance theater.
- [[Build the Checker Before the Generator]] — For constrained output, write the deterministic validator first and watch it fail; it defines done and turns failures into repair instructions.
- [[Trend on Occurrence Date, Not Run Date]] — Bucket recurring findings by when the cited code was last touched; bursty run dates make every group read as rising.
- [[Deterministic Skill-Candidate Detection]] — Detect skill candidates with a provenance-carrying tool-sequence n-gram counter; defer the LLM to the human-gated retro.
- [[Re-Derive, Never Re-Read]] — A status field saying "met" is a claim; verify by re-running the command it names, since recorded prose decays.
- [[Due-vs-Acked Trigger Counters]] — Hand triggers across sessions as cumulative due/acked file counters, acking only after verifying the action landed.
- [[Telemetry Claim Verification Gate]] — Recompute every dashboard number from the raw corpus and assign a verdict before it may drive a hook or rule.
- [[Analysis Gap vs Collection Gap]] — Before building a collector, check whether the data exists and only analysis or grain is missing; reject partial-coverage collectors.
- [[No Evidence Ranks Below Confident Mismatch]] — When a scoring dimension gives 0 both for "no evidence" and for "evidence that contradicts the request", and an uncertainty penalty then fires only on the missing-evidence case, an unknown item ranks below a confidently wrong one. Make the two states structurally different before arguing about weights.
- [[Build the Knobs, Not the Choice]] — When a scoring question splits into defects you can decide from the definition and weighting questions only a benchmark can settle, ship the defects now and build the weighting options as a registry of switchable variants with a control arm identical to current behaviour. The benchmark then picks a variant that already exists.
- [[Parsed Then Dropped at the Insert Mapper]] — A crawl parses price, hours and descriptions, keeps them in memory through normalisation, then silently discards them in the typed insert mapper because the Insert type mirrors the table — and a natural-key merge discards the losing listing's fields the same way, so the data is computed and unrecoverable.
- [[Mutation Score Artifact from Zero-Test Runs]] — A mutation-testing runner that selects tests by joined name can silently run zero tests for describe-nested suites, so every mutant reads as Survived and the score measures test style, not test strength — check that each mutant actually ran tests before reading any score.
- [[Validator That Proves by Cast]] — A hand-written validator that returns its input "as T" after checking less than T claims gives a type that implies validation which never happened — measure failure counts report-only, then move request bodies to schema parsing, and benchmark before parsing database rows.
- [[Intake Option Separability via Reach Vectors]] — Measure whether intake options are actually different requests by taking the cosine of each option's reach vector over the tag space; the options that look distinct often form a clique, and because coverage and separation both flow through the same adjacency graph, the lever is pruning adjacency edges, not splitting options.
- [[Single-Subject End-to-End Trace]] — Walk one real subject (one venue) through every pipeline stage with a PASS/FAIL row per stage, running the real stage functions — it surfaces gates nobody can pass, rows that sit in no queue, and instruments that shipped green but were never armed, which per-stage tests and aggregate dashboards both miss.
- [[Input-Starved Enrichment]] — When an enrichment prompt gets no descriptive text, the model returns empty tags and the item scores zero on every dimension that reads them — check what the payload actually contains before tuning the prompt, and never feed provenance fields like operator notes as content.
- [[Hashed Fingerprint Contamination Gate]] — Prove a new repo excludes given material: hashed normalized lines (publishable), local vocabulary-set overlap and numeric-profile checks, calibrated by flagging the contaminated old tree first
- [[Stated Seams Drift, Enforced Seams Hold]] — Two repos built from the same architecture refs diverge exactly where one enforces a seam in lint/CI and the other only describes it in CLAUDE.md — a stated layer arrow is broken within weeks, a stated "one model seam" is bypassed by judges, a stated failure path fabricates success — so every architectural rule needs an enforcing check with an empty exception list.
- [[Layered Ingestion Failure Posture]] — An ingestion pipeline needs a different failure response at each grain — repair-or-drop a bad field, quarantine a bad entity, warn on a dead feed, fail closed on an empty build, fail open loudly when the issue reporter itself breaks — and detectors should write to an issue store with a closed vocabulary that deep-links to the fix site, not to logs.
- [[Admit at Low Prior, Don't Exclude]] — Before reading a catalogue representation baseline, write the out-of-scope rule — a low-count kind is excluded only if it is structurally unsurfaceable AND exclusion is no more reversible than admitting it — because an admitted row at a low prior keeps the measurement possible while an excluded row can never be measured again.
- [[Size the Chain to Its Join Key]] — Before building a multi-slice enrichment chain, measure what fraction of real rows carry the join key it hangs off — six tested slices were built on a Wikidata QID that only 2.9% of production rows have, while the same field was on 86% of rows via a key the pipeline already held and dropped — and point fixtures at real database values, since tests that agree with each other can all disagree with production.
- [[Score-Delta Retry Guard]] — Stop a retrieval retry before the model is called when the new top score is not meaningfully better than the previous one (curr_top ≤ prev_top + ε, ε = 0.05). That saves 1–2 s and one LLM call on dead-end queries, at the cost of a fixed threshold that occasionally skips a small but useful improvement.
- [[Gate Pass Rate Measures Effort, Not Readiness]] — A readiness gate whose own pass rate is high says nothing about whether its output is ready. 81% of triaged issues reached READY, but READY issues passed their first build review only 16% of the time, against 25% for untriaged ones. The discriminating number is a downstream join, not anything the gate's own ledger records.
- [[Plan-Insufficient Is Not Blocked]] — A build loop needs a terminal outcome that means "the plan is wrong", separate from "the code has a blocker". Fixing code against an unsound plan produces a green review of the wrong change, and folding the two outcomes together deletes the only signal that tells upstream planning its output was inadequate.
- [[Contract-Anchored Findings Get Fixed]] — Across 238 review findings, the ones citing a rule the repo had already written down were fixed 9 of 9 with no pushback. Other findings averaged 65%, and the dimension furthest from any written rule ran 44%. So every review dimension should be able to cite the change contract, not only the contracts reviewer.
- [[Natural Key Without a Discriminator Collapses Rows]] — An ingest upsert that matches on a natural key missing its discriminator (venue + title, but not date) silently merges distinct occurrences. One source would have collapsed ~1,800 dated occurrences into ~1,000 rows, and with no snapshot store the damage is repairable only by re-fetching everything, so the key must be fixed before the catalogue grows.
- [[State Writer Never Owns Its Side Effects]] — one sole writer per lifecycle field; a DB webhook fans the committed change out to comms, so every write yields identical notifications and new consumers touch zero producers
- [[Paired Allow Test for Deny Rules]] — each threat vector runs as a fail-closed test paired with the legitimate operation that must succeed, so deny-all can't pass; the threat model transfers across stacks, the harness doesn't
- [[Hint Ladder With Logged Aid Rung]] — five announced help rungs; the highest rung used is logged as Aid, so help changes what's offered, never what's recorded
- [[Voice-Preserving Redraft Loop]] — name the phrases to keep first, flag invented sentences, record the person's words verbatim, promote style notes on second occurrence
- [[Verify a Render by Text Extents, Not Pixels]] — silent-clipping PDF renders verified via pdftotext -bbox extents and keyword probes, not raster scans or page count
- [[Consequence-Flagged KB Audit]] — audit every help-center article into topic / must-be-exact facts / conditions / financial-consequence flag before indexing; surfaces conflicting, duplicate, stale, placeholder and non-article defects plus 'must not imply' negative constraints
- [[Provenance-Tiered Confidence Gate]] — with no reranker score, derive confidence from which source answered; collapse tiers no reader branches on; only the bottom tier triggers an honest non-answer
- [[Mocked-Away Body Hides the Defect]] — a test that patches the unit itself never runs its body; mock one level below, and treat rare-branch and fire-and-forget code as untested
- [[Execution-Made Decisions Leave Stale Invariants]] — When execution silently reverses a plan's resolved decision, the invariant sentence outlives it; re-run the plan's test-plan grep as a drift detector, scope the fix as reconciliation, and treat same-named files as different documents until content proves a copy.
- [[Warranted vs Executed Escalation]] — attribute escalation signals to user vs agent side; only agent-side signals mean "executed", user-side ones tell the judge escalation was warranted
- [[Measurement Fix Mistaken for Model Gain]] — citation/ground-truth identifier mismatch makes correct answers score as failures; report v1-strict vs v2-calibrated and treat baselines as lower bounds
- [[Every Registration Site Needs a Guard]] — When one closed-set member must be mirrored in N places, guard every site, mutation-test each guard, and assert completeness where the set is consumed; an arbiter test covering two of five sites gives false confidence.
- [[Tested Decision, Untested Expression]] — Tests cover the decision but not its expression or wiring; a well-tested refusal guard was invisible in three of five report locations because string-anchor injections silently matched nothing.
- [[Worktree Agent Dispatch Contract]] — the dispatcher owns git; create the worktree in the target repo, branch from committed state with --no-track, and treat agent commits as provisional
- [[Issues Live in the Repo They Change]] — file issues where the code changes, not on a central board; fully qualify #N refs before gh issue transfer
- [[Comparable Tables, Not One Source]] — keep per-stage vocabulary tables separate and pin their agreement with a test; merging would hide a real cross-stage disagreement
- [[Itinerary as Anchored Slot Composition]] — multi-stop plans built as anchor + slot template + exact enumeration; the model only parses (LLM-only planners fail constraints >90% of the time)
- [[A Sourcing Gap Is Not an Effect Problem]] — a working parallax-clip effect rejected because the wanted content (interiors) was missing; measure sources and licence policies before building
- [[A Check Must Say Why It Could Not Check]] — a gate must distinguish UNCHECKED-and-why from FAILED; an undiagnosed red points at the wrong repair, and escape hatches must be tested where the real check would run
- [[Settings Field Names Are a Shared Env Namespace]] — pydantic-settings binds fields to same-named env vars, so sibling Settings classes collide silently; prefix per component and audit siblings for collisions and drift
- [[Execute Every Toggle Configuration]] — render and execute every toggle combination of a scaffold, not just the default, because untested configurations ship broken
- [[Verify CI Steps by Extraction, Not Retyping]] — verify CI by extracting and running the real workflow steps rather than retyping them locally
- [[Gate Coupled Features on the Producer, Not the Label]] — gate dependent scaffold features on the component that produces their input, not a correlated project label
- [[Circular Core Justification]] — an always-on core whose components justify only each other is a demo welded in as infrastructure
- [[Capability Catalog with Preconditions]] — declared requires/sets/conflicts_with catalog answers "what can still be added to this repo?"
- [[Mislabelled Column Is Worse Than Missing]] — a populated column that stores a different quantity than its name says is worse than a missing one; rename it to what it holds and add a correctly named column
- [[Advance-Only Machine Writes on a Shared Lifecycle]] — the machine writer advances only from states it owns, never to published, never over human decisions; the guard is a WHERE filter on the UPDATE
- [[Guard at the Funnel, Fix at the Cause]] — a plausibility bound at the single return funnel, a fix to the extractor at the cause, and drop-or-flag policy kept at the importer, with shared constants
- [[Publish Automatically, Sample for Review]] — a publish-time random sample graded pass/fail by an operator, with each failure attributed to a pipeline stage, instead of gating every item
- [[Hedging Belongs in the Gate, Not the Answer]] — correct but long, hedged suggestions get ignored; route low confidence to the gate and answer directly
- [[Borderline-Band Sufficiency Probe]] — keep fast score gates, spend a cheap LLM sufficiency check only in a two-threshold uncertainty band
- [[Verification Thins Where Blast Radius Widens]] — in a self-improving agent loop, the widest-reach changes (global rules, hooks, skills) had only a routing-only eval that skipped behavioural cases and still reported 99% pass; rank verification by what a change can break
- [[Reproduce to Tell a Break From a Stuck Agent]] — re-run from the recorded state to tell a real defect from harness exhaustion or a stalled agent
- [[Observed Transitions, Not Declared]] — validate plan-status transitions from the observed before/after state, not the edit's self-declared From: field
- [[Read-Side Retraction by Observer Vintage]] — stamp observer versions on append-only ledger rows so defective observers are retracted by selection, never by editing history
- [[Decision-Seat Inventory for a Control Plane]] — enumerate who decides each transition before building an orchestrator; most seats stay human, making it a control plane
- [[Artifact-Canonical Stateless Driver]] — a driver that re-derives all state from on-disk artifacts each step, holding nothing in memory
- [[Map Every State to Its Writer]] — every enum state needs a named writer; a state nothing writes is a dead terminal or a missing exit
- [[In-Band Stream Errors Read as Bad Answers]] — streaming protocols report failures as error chunks in a 200 stream; a text-only parser scores them as 0.00 answers
- [[Toggle Gates the Capability, Not the Runtime Default]] — a toggle decides whether the code path exists; flipping an eagerly-read default breaks every import
- [[CI Tests the Merge Ref, Not the Branch Tip]] — a default changed on main turns a branch red in CI but not locally; reproduce on the merge ref, collapse legs to one root cause
- [[Verify Merge State by Content, Not Label]] — labels, commit messages and ahead-counts are stale caches; decide what landed by grepping main for the change
- [[Skip Steps, Not Jobs, Behind a Path Filter]] — path-filter CI by gating steps, not jobs, so checks report success rather than skipped; verify negated filters with a mixed commit and recheck every 'gate always runs' invariant
- [[Proxy Schema Overfits the Guess]] — a schema guessed before the real source arrives hardens into required fields; a strict never-drop validator then rejects every real row
- [[Vendored Hooks Resolve to the Global Copy]] — $CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR through a symlinked .claude runs global hooks, so repo-local hook edits do nothing; fix canon first, keep byte-identical, check parity
- [[Adversarially Independent Baseline Arm]] — build the naive comparison arm on the same data contract, differing only in aggregation, so it can genuinely win
- [[Async Seam Before the Table]] — put an async, fixture-backed interface in front of data that has no table yet; null must mean one thing
- [[Printed URL Is a Public Contract]] — a URL printed on QR boards is frozen: keep locale out of the path, derive origin from request headers, guard localhost in code
- [[Unfinished Work Must Fail Closed]] — a partial artifact whose "do not apply" warning is a comment applies cleanly and reports success; make the first executable statement a guard that refuses to run
- [[Degrade the Render, Surface the Failure]] — when enrichment fails on top of loaded core data, keep rendering and log to a sink operators read; throw only when continuing could record a wrong outcome
- [[Layout Membership by Location]] — decide chrome by nested route group rather than a pathname conditional, then guard the file location because it is now the invariant
- [[Audit the Class, Not the Instance]] — sweep every surface behind the same access boundary before fixing; a silent read bug was masking a loud write bug on a second page
- [[Adoption Ban Needs an Escape Hatch]] — a lint ban with no primitive for every banned case forces misuse of the nearest primitive; test every behaviour that justified the dependency
- [[Capability Token in a Serialized Row]] — when a bearer token doubles as a row field, passing whole rows to a client component serializes every credential into the page; aggregate server-side and sign the cookie
- [[Deadline Transition Computed on Read]] — open/closed after a deadline is a shared pure predicate evaluated on read, not a scheduled writer
- [[Event–Occurrence Split, Added Behind the Old Column]] — separate events from dated occurrences additively, keep the old column as a maintained copy; fix not rebuild when rows are parity evidence
- [[Fixture Eval Blind to the Swapped Component]] — a fixture eval that never calls the model scores 1.0 for any model or prompt; gate model swaps on a live sample
- [[Single Writer, Offline Consolidation]] — When several in-session skills each rewrite the same long-lived memory files, per-event transformation decays into append; the fix the field converged on is capture-only during the session and one offline consolidator as the sole writer, with every output wired to exactly one consumer.
- [[Test-Gated Auto-Fix with Per-Hunk Revert]] — An agent may apply review findings itself only if the repo has a working test harness. It tiers each finding by blast radius, applies only low-radius fixes, one at a time, runs the tests after each, and reverts the hunk on failure. It runs in the main session, never in a subagent, and never commits.
- [[De-Dupe Before You Normalize]] — Before rolling a new convention across N copies of something, classify each copy as COPY, REPO-SPECIFIC or DIVERGED-COPY. In one audit about 120 skill instances turned out to be six clean repos, one intentional template and two drift sites, so normalizing first would have mostly polished stale duplicates.
- [[Rate Metrics Measure the Logger]] — When what gets recorded depends on the instrumentation setup (a session wrote a note only if it compacted), population-rate metrics measure the logger and cannot be trended across a setup change. Make the regime an explicit column, trend only per-row properties, and check each field's meaning with one query before building a chart on it.
- [[Harness Events Are Not Task Failures]] — When classifying agent sessions or errors from transcripts, permission prompts, interrupts and tool-plumbing errors are harness events, not task failures. Success needs a positive fact, otherwise the outcome is unknown, and errors are attributed by who owns the fix. The headline count is not the signal; its composition is.
- [[Conservation Gate on Accumulator Clear]] — When an agent consolidates an inbox and then clears it, a blocking hook checks a conservation law: if N entries left the inbox, at least N disposition rows (retained, merged or discarded, with a reason) must have landed in a ledger. Clearing becomes auditable without judging the prose.
- [[Pipeline-Absorbed Skills]] — A well-written skill can get zero invocations because a dominant skill pipeline intercepts the user's upstream intent, and free-text requests are either absorbed by that pipeline or answered inline. When two description rewrites change nothing, the cause is structural: wire the skill into the pipeline's footers and merge any hidden prerequisite chain.
- [[Config Reference Rot]] — Agent and CI config refers to things by name: tools, commands, paths, list members. When the referent is renamed, deleted or added, the config stays valid and silently stops doing its job. Guard every such list in both directions, and check paths when the file is written.
- [[Readiness Is Pinned to a Tree State]] — a plan's READY holds only against the tree and decisions it was checked against; re-earn it against the live tree after every dependency lands
- [[The Run Record Belongs to the Process]] — the scheduled process inserts its own run row, holds the id, closes it on SIGTERM within a budget, asserts the UPDATE matched, and the reader marks stale after timeout
- [[The Free Decision Is the Long-Lead Item]] — when a $0 decision gates fast steps that gate an unacceleratable calendar clock, the decision is the long-lead item; some decisions expire at first irreversible use
- [[One-Off Codemod, Permanent Gate]] — throwaway codemod plus permanent lint gate for token migration; verify built output, keep the map versioned, re-review every fix
- [[Model Gateway Is Transport, Call Site Owns Meaning]] — one lint-enforced provider seam that stays pure transport (no error wrapping, no spend ledger); review defects are all call-site meaning dropped on the way through
- [[A Capped Run Is Not a Clean Run]] — a working per-item spend stop that exits 0 makes truncation read green; distinct exit code, grep-able run signal, and a costed-batch protocol (verified prerequisite, true count, paid sample, kill condition)
- [[Landed Is Not Complete]] — a gate that awaits the log write proves a row landed, not that it is complete or joinable; probe production, split at the migration, report N for conditional checks
- [[Atomic Archive-Then-Delete for Irreversible Retention]] — copy-and-delete in one writable-CTE statement behind a DB-level row ceiling, app pre-flight and post-call delta assertion; dry-run default and typed-confirm manual dispatch
- [[Done-When the Cheapest Write Can Meet]] — a done-when naming a downstream stage's intake is met by the cheapest direct insert, which may be unservable; tighten it to the property that implies servability and feed the existing writer
- [[Attribution Inherited From the Source Row]] — taking an attribute from the source row instead of per record silently mislabels every record when one source serves many entities
- [[Insert-Only Upserts Strand Normalizer Fixes]] — once upserts are insert-only, normalizer fixes reach new rows only; name the route to old rows (dry-run backfill that overwrites only known-bad defaults)
- [[Expected-Failure Tests Pass for Any Throw]] — an it.fails trip-wire went green on a broken seed; assert today's buggy outcome with a plain test instead
- [[Live-Database Test Harness Hazards]] — prod-delete risk via unguarded client paths, cleanup that swallows leaks, probes returning 200 for junk keys, and cleanup-between isolation when rollback is unreachable through PostgREST
- [[Make Each RLS Policy Individually Falsifiable]] — test each policy through its governed role, distinguish WITH CHECK errors from USING zero-row filters, import the app predicate, probe pg_class not the migration tracker
- [[A Secret Scanner Must Never Echo Its Match]] — client-bundle secret gate reporting file:line only, pinned by a self-defeat test, detecting via decoded JWT role claim
- [[Re-Crawl Adds, Never Overwrites]] — once a content row exists, a re-crawl only adds occurrences/dates and flags real content changes for review; same-URL overwrites rename, hide and revert published rows and break enrichment skip-gates
- [[Server Shell, Client Leaf]] — Move Next.js layouts/forms off use client: resolve data server-side, extract the smallest hook island, keep the old fetch as an optional-prop fallback
- [[Single-Definition Grep Guard]] — Prove a rule is defined once with a fixed-string grep test; avoid BRE escaping, exit-1, and hand-rolled-equivalent traps
- [[Render-Blind Test Layer]] — jsdom cannot see contrast or tap-target defects; guard them with Playwright boundingBox or Lighthouse, not an axe test that cannot fail
- [[Name-Only Sync Guards Miss Content Drift]] — a vendoring guard that checks listed names misses machine-local paths, orphans, unvendored references and stale indexes; a repairing sync needs a --check mode
- [[Hash the Frame as Fitted, Not the Source File]] — run-record data identity must fingerprint the post-derivation matrix; a source-file hash certifies the very discrepancy it should catch
- [[Changing a Derived Default Is a Migration]] — Copier re-derives `when: false` variables on every update, so a default change re-lays-out existing consumers; ship a pin-the-old-value release note
- [[Client-Error Reporting Seam Is an Anonymous Log Sink]] — a browser error-report route writes attacker-controlled free text into logs; size caps, no stacks, three fixed fields, fingerprint dedupe and a rate limit from commit one, and not done until something reads the stream
- [[Empty-String Env Override Defeats Nullish Coalescing]] — `VAR= cmd` makes a var present-but-empty, so `env.VAR ?? fallback` keeps the empty string; use `||` for env fallbacks and `delete process.env.X` for one-off bypasses
- [[Argued Carve-Out with a Stop Condition]] — When a layering rule flags code that has exactly one caller and no consumer wanting anything different, the right fix can be a written exception where the rule lives — named file, reason, and the condition that would end it — not a new abstraction (reporter injection, generators) built for a consumer that doesn't exist.
- [[Dated Node Must Name the Row]] — an attribute borrowed from a page sub-node (exhibition end date on a museum row) is kept only on an exact name match, containment deliberately excluded; null dates fail toward serving
- [[Production Write Guard: Explicit Override, Never Ambient]] — print the target at the shared client factory and refuse non-local writes without a deliberate flag; CI=true rejected as an ambient bypass; caller-check scheduled writers first
- [[Env Inventory Gate Enforced in One Direction]] — one spec table of env vars parsed by a gate that fails on undocumented reads (file:line) but only notes reader-less rows; git pathspec src/**/*.ts silently skipped src/ root files
- [[Widen a Capped Vocabulary Without Displacing]] — merging a richer keyword list into a capped selector crowded real links out of the top N; rank legacy matches first so new terms only fill leftover slots
- [[Default-Deny Guard With a Named Allowlist]] — guards scan the whole tree and deny by default; exceptions are named in config at value granularity with a reason, never hardcoded lists or file-level exemptions
- [[Baseline Only a Decided, Deterministic State]] — commit visual-regression baselines only for decided, deterministic UI: CI-Linux generated, time-masked, captured after layout fixes land
- [[Write-Time Deterministic Holdout]] — decide a signal's train/benchmark side at insert time as a hash of item identity stored on the row; re-saves never flip sides and each consumer enforces it with one WHERE clause
- [[Indistinguishable Rejection on Best-Effort Sinks]] — drop forged telemetry ids with the same 202 as a capacity drop; then assert payloads, since wiring regressions go silent too
- [[Characterization Pins Before Extraction]] — pin an untested script's output against its original blob, mutation-prove the pins, default generalized policy to today's behaviour
- [[Bounded Slice After In-Memory Eligibility]] — fetch fully (explicit pagination past implicit row caps), order with a pure function, truncate candidates but never the skip accounting
- [[Observe-Mode Gate With a Named Enforce Trigger]] — ship a strict gate observing, fail only on today's invariant, flip to enforce on a countable condition
- [[Dropped Filter Flips the Truncation Direction]] — moving a filter out of SQL changes which rows ORDER BY/LIMIT drops
- [[Known-Gap Alarms Ship as Warn]] — health-check alarms already true for tracked reasons ship at warn behind an escalation tunable; the fixer reuses the alarm's predicate; escalate only after the first verified fix
- [[Batch Path Through the Synchronous Chain]] — async batch mode reuses the synchronous chain via one precomputed-output field, proved by a differential test and a single-writer spy
- [[Fixing an Accidentally Load-Bearing Bug]] — before closing a gap, ask what it was silently rescuing; ship the fix with an explicit release-valve state
- [[Widen a Narrow Type, Re-Home Its Constraints]] — widening a deliberately narrow row type means re-homing each implicit rule as an explicit per-kind branch
- [[Backfill Without the Forward Path]] — fix the writer first, then a dry-run-default backfill with re-query, then ratchet the gate
- [[Place Modules by Consumer, Not by Name]] — shared layer only for no-I/O substrate of several consumers; name folders for the data cluster they write
- [[A Partial Fix of a Duplicated Doc Is Worse Than None]] — correcting one copy of a duplicated contract turns agreement into disagreement; sync both or retire one
- [[Keep the Source Subtype Before the First Collapse]] — find the first lossy step on the live write path (not the bucketing the issue names), keep the raw upstream identity as a namespaced fact column with a prefix-only CHECK, and fill an already-read-but-never-written column before adding a new one
- [[Arm Unattended Writers by Risk Type]] — Ship scheduled writers disarmed in a way that matches their risk: commented-out cron for spend, dry-run-unless-confirmed for destruction, a repo-variable step guard inside a workflow that must keep running
- [[Resumable Scheduled Batch Sweep]] — Each scheduled run either resolves the one open batch job or submits one bounded slice; no auto-retry without a lineage cap, and separate spend ledgers
- [[Behaviour-Preserving Test Suite Split]] — Split oversized suites along describe seams with one sibling fixtures module, hold the runner-reported per-subsystem test count as the contract (it.each makes grep counts wrong), and ship any stateful property model with a deliberately wrong expectation that must fail.
- [[Review Stamp Moves Only When the Verdict Does]] — Server-stamp reviewed-at only when an axis actually changed, keep evidence on revert to needs-review, and give privileged columns their own named write action, because re-review triggers read the timestamp.
- [[Follow-Up Step Needs a Derived Worklist]] — A follow-up form shown only in the render after a status flip is pruned by the revalidated query; list the next step from a persistent anti-join, which also stops offering duplicates.
- [[Coalesce Against a Never-Null Source]] — `coalesce(incoming, existing)` only protects a field when the incoming side can actually be null.
- [[Risk-Weighted Coverage Floors]] — Enforce coverage as per-directory floors on the riskiest code, each set just below the current measurement and proven able to fail, rather than one repository-wide percentage that averages weak areas into strong ones.
- [[Component Test in a Shape the App Never Uses]] — A dialog's 'restores focus on close' test stayed green while focus fell to <body> in every real dialog, because the fixture used the uncontrolled Trigger shape, the only shape where the library restores focus itself, and the app only used controlled dialogs.
- [[One Use Case Behind Two Transports]] — When an HTTP route and a Server Action each implement the same command, they drift and each one inherits the same non-atomic write.
- [[Fail-Closed E2E Journey Proof]] — An e2e test with a try/catch, success flag or conditional skip around its key step can report green without ever saving anything.
- [[Recency-Seeded Label Queue Samples the Thin End]] — a label queue seeded from the most recent rows samples the emptiest ones; seed only judgeable rows, stratify kind × field with a per-cell cap, rank by rater basis
- [[Branch Gated on State Only It Writes]] — a branch gated on stored state whose only writer is that branch never fires; gate on a precondition another path produces and test from an empty store
- [[Count What the Merge Already Folded]] — corroboration as count(distinct host) over groups an upstream natural-key merge already formed; floor of 1 is neutral, never a penalty
- [[Brand the Funnel's Output]] — a nominal brand with one producer enforces that callers route through a shaping function; unions cannot express provenance
- [[Triage Casts by Mechanical Probe]] — regenerate the type oracle, then probe `as unknown as` → `as` / `satisfies` to sort casts into delete, zod adapter, or keep-with-reason
- [[Name the Rollback, Then Test It]] — extract optimistic updates into hooks with named applyOptimistic/rollback so the undo path gets tested
- [[Offline Pipeline Seed Harness]] — drive real stage functions over pinned fixtures; throwing fetcher + fetch stub catch unthreaded seams; date-shift at the seam, ZERO-YIELD/STARVED counter report, vacuous idempotency on empty tables
- [[Foreign Rows in a Shared Log Table]] — a second surface logging into another's table needs a discriminator every reader filters on, child tables scoped via parent ids, and failed parent reads shown as unknown, not zero
- [[Review Preview Over the Production Pipeline]] — Review an unpublished backlog through the production ranker and card via a sibling candidate source, a split visibility predicate, a preview log surface with every reader audited, and verdict-not-publish approval.
- [[URL-Carried Rationale Is Forged Provenance]] — A per-serve "why this fits you" line cannot ride a query parameter to a canonical page: forgeable, leaks through share, and blocked if the log stores scores not signals.
- [[Queue Gap Is Mechanical Before It Is Curatorial]] — Decompose a review-queue gap by mechanical cause before calling it curation; persist exclusions invisibly, give probe verdicts a DB state before scheduling, and keep operator taste in the queue, not the ranker.
- [[Snapshot the Judged Value on the Verdict Row]] — human review rows for pre-canonical or mutable subjects store an immutable snapshot of what was judged, seeded insert-only, cleared not deleted
- [[Edit In Place by Diffing on the Natural Key]] — edit a parent with cascading user-owned children by diffing on the natural key in one transactional function; delete-all/reinsert silently orphans votes
- [[A Second Write Path Meets the First Path's Hidden Gates]] — a new ingestion path calling the write primitive directly hits CHECK/NOT NULL/allowlist constraints the old path met by construction; turn silent drops into hard upfront validation
- [[Vacuous Check Turns Backwards on Policy Change]] — a check passing on an empty population can encode a reversed policy and fail the right behaviour on the first qualifying row; re-read predicates when a decision amends a meaning
- [[Prompt Re-Asks What the Gate Proved]] — a prompt question restating a precheck rule re-verifies a fact already certain; render model-owned checks only, test for re-duplication
- [[Read-Side Schema Strips the New Field]] — a reader's strict object schema drops an undeclared new field, so tooling reports 0 yield over populated data
- [[Edge Registry With a Completeness-Checked Manifest]] — consolidate drifting intent-to-vocabulary maps into one neutral module with a manifest asserted complete against its exports
- [[Widening a Postgres Function Creates an Overload]] — create or replace with extra defaulted params adds a second overload; drop the old signature first
- [[Persist Paid Work Per Chunk]] — embed a chunk, upsert it atomically, record failures, skip by input hash, exit non-zero on any failure
- [[SSR Auth Fan-Out]] — proxy, layout and page each calling getUser; measure per phase on a deployed build, collapse to one cached call, skip for cookie-less requests
- [[Dead Modifier Trace]] — a modifier reading 0 everywhere is plumbing (second constructor, mismatched spelling) or real absence; trace inputs per pair before fixing
- [[Pure Derivation Is a Generated Column]] — a value derived purely from the row (lexical tsvector) belongs in a generated column, not a side table; probe function volatility (IMMUTABLE vs STABLE) in a rolled-back transaction first
- [[The Eval Harness Has Its Own Adapter]] — a modifier dead only in eval traced to a second, harness-local row-to-candidate adapter reading a bucketed field; fix at the harness boundary and prove with a synthetic fixture when the corpus lacks the field
- [[Required Field as Exhaustive Site Finder]] — add a new field as required so the compiler lists every construction site, cross-check with a sibling-field grep, and never make it optional to silence a compile error
- [[Fixed-Rule Clusters With a Degeneracy Gate]] — group a small enriched corpus by named rule hypotheses, reject degenerate shapes by construction, gate the shape check with hand-authored fixtures, and keep un-enriched items in a labelled group
- [[Verbatim Evidence Quote Verification]] — Require a verbatim evidence quote per scored tag, verify it per evidence block after parsing, and drop unverifiable tags through the existing drop channel
- [[Closed Judge Vocabulary, Tolerant Parse]] — Close a judge's free-text issue list to canonical codes via a synonym map and an `other` valve, without enum-parse brittleness
- [[Freeze the Bucketing Key Across Identifier Rotation]] — A recomputed holdout split must key on a frozen copy of the old hash, not a pseudonym you plan to rotate
- [[A Serialized Blob Is a Prompt Contract]] — A JSON column stringified into a judge prompt makes its shape a prompt contract; find pass-through readers before reshaping
- [[Count the Violation, Not the Token]] — Size a boundary refactor by clustering grep hits against layer rules, not by token count; record a false premise as premise_false
- [[Silent Join Miss Reads as a Finding]] — key-format drift or null coercion makes a join miss silently, and the miss fallback reads as a real result
- [[Idempotency Lives on the Link Column, Not the Synthetic Key]] — re-runnable backfills guard on the subject link, not a sequence-defaulted unique key
- [[Quality-Gate Dashboard in Pipeline Order]] — stage-ordered admin dashboard with product/technical strips, failure attribution and eval-gate reason classes
- [[Skip Keyed on Config, Not Capability]] — a test skip gated on env-var presence instead of importability hid 14 tests as green-by-omission; probe the import, floor on zero skips, and run a reviewer's prescribed fix before adopting it
- [[A Sentinel Catches Forgetting, Not Asserting]] — typed NotApplicable with null-rejection blocks omission but a false reason round-trips cleanly; grep for consumers before tagging a field not-applicable
- [[A Check That Answers a Proxy Question]] — a capability precheck verified a file existed, not that the session registry would resolve it; emit the scope the check answered (absolute root, cwd)
- [[Argued Empty List]] — an empty exclusion/findings list must carry its assumption, a considered-and-kept set, and a revisit trigger, or review cannot tell checked-and-empty from never-checked
- [[Do-Instead-Nothing Rule Silences a Loud Denial]] — a Postgres `do instead nothing` rule runs before the permission check, turning a loud 42501 into a silent 0-row success that grep tests and has_table_privilege probes cannot see
- [[Page on a Total Order]] — range pagination is only correct on a unique ORDER BY; non-unique keys drop or repeat rows at page boundaries, and fakes that ignore .range() hide it
- [[Strict Parsers for Alarm Config]] — alerting-tool parsers must refuse, not fall back (vacuous zero thresholds, unknown ack ids, lenient booleans), with exit codes 0/1/2 separating alarm from check failure
- [[A Constraint Cannot Tell a Regression From a Legacy Null]] — when legacy rows are legitimately NULL, a NOT NULL CHECK cannot catch a write-path regression and only rejects valid writes; defer it until a backfill
- [[Audit-Don't-Assert Release Checklist]] — release checklist whose rows are true/false/error, where error (could not check) is never folded into a pass
- [[HITL Gradient]] — four attendance levels (L1–L4) for agent runs, with seats cited from a seat table and divergence routed to named destinations
- [[Hoist Irreversible Decisions to the Attended Band]] — move one-way decisions into the band where a human is present; an unwatched async channel is a timeout with extra steps
- [[Dispatcher Carries the Skill Body]] — disable-model-invocation hides skills from subagents too, so the dispatcher reads the body fresh, substitutes $ARGUMENTS, and grants allowed-tools
- [[Verify Ignore Rules Against a Real File]] — gitignore/check-ignore/nbstripout/pre-commit gotchas that let a test pass over a broken rule
- [[Control Plane Owns the Join, Not the Truth]] — a work item spans authoritative subdomains; the control plane reconciles them (agree / disagree / could-not-join) and never caches their truth
- [[Unit Tests Encode the Checker's Assumption]] — 65 findings, 0 true positives, 20/20 green: only live-corpus spot-checks, pin-then-invert tests and hook mutation prove a checker
- [[Hardcoded Dashboard Cells Are Expiring Claims]] — hand-typed numbers on dashboards and docs go stale within hours; tier static content, render never-run, alert on recency and breadth
- [[Review Model Tiering by Breadth and Severity Bias]] — model tiers differ in review breadth and severity bias; mid tier by default, top tier on costly-corruption paths, multi-model agreement elevates findings
- [[Plan Step Done Requires a Commit]] — a deterministic verify-commits gate checks each DONE plan step against git log before the plan can close
- [[Dispatch-Shaped Agents Hide Cost]] — mechanism-named subagents left ~90% of cost unattributed; role contracts for the top workflows, and attribution that never guesses
- [[Dark Outcome Tier]] — the outcome tier went unmeasured while easy proxies were tuned; pilot an outcome signal by hand and apply the action test to every metric
- [[Idempotent Reconcile Beside a Guarded Create]] — keep create guarded and non-idempotent; link/update via an append-only reconcile that only adds what is missing
- [[Ambient Tool Resolution Phantom]] — uv run of an undeclared tool resolves an ambient install, producing phantom parity drift; gate on the declaration and suspect the check first
- [[Format Contract Lives in the Parser]] — model-written logs are really specified by downstream positional regexes; drift renders as "no data", so migrate consumer-first
- [[Byte-Diffable Vendor-and-Move Migration]] — move a subsystem across repos by vendoring a byte-identical subset, deferring fixes, overlapping captures, deleting only after a verified scheduled run
- [[Single Typed Verdict, Consumers Never Re-Derive]] — a merge verdict implemented three times drifts; derive it once in a pure typed function exposed as a CLI
- [[Category Is a Bucket, Not a Recurrence Signature]] — recurrence needs named cross-category signatures, durable finding ids, and a log window longer than the threshold
- [[Monitor the Gate, Not the Stage]] — per-gate fire rate, precision with n (`unlabelled` when no verdicts), recall proxy and drift decide whether an unattended pipeline's flags can be trusted
- [[Orchestrate Around the Human Gate]] — triage/build/meta orchestrators that chain stage scouts and halt at the human gate with resumable, file-derived state
- [[Per-Row Content Hash Misses Cross-Row State]] — incremental indexes gated on a row hash miss backlink counts, embedding-model drift, FTS and deletions
- [[Cumulative Metric Hides the Fix]] — an all-time bucket metric with an unfixable historical cause makes a working fix read as failure; split by cause, measure windowed
- [[Verify the Issue's Premise Before Planning]] — issue bodies are pre-inspection hypotheses; four of five load-bearing premises were false and each correction changed the plan
- [[Paired Bugs That Cancel]] — an id-keyed dedup dropping findings masked a repair pass duplicating them; fix together and add a conservation test
- [[Caller Default Shadows the Resolver]] — When a resolver correctly lets an explicit argument win (`opts.model ?? resolved`), any caller that fills an unset flag with a hard-coded default turns \"unset\" into \"explicit\" and the provider-aware resolver is never consulted — fix the callers by routing them through the one resolver helper, not by weakening the precedence rule.
- [[Cross-Surface Order Parity Test]] — When one ranking ("look at this next") is shown on several surfaces, move the derivation and its input readers into one shared module, extract a pure scoring function from each surface, and assert all of them produce the same relative order over one fixture set — a key mismatch or a forgotten input then fails a test instead of quietly reordering one screen.
- [[Tighten a Visibility Gate by Backfilling the Visible State]] — When a new review-status column tightens a public read policy from `using (true)`, backfill existing rows to the state that keeps them visible and default only new rows to draft — otherwise the migration silently 404s every live public page, and nothing in the diff reads as the cause; find the anon-scoped readers by grepping every call site against its client constructor.
